For coordinator-backed and direct cloud providers it releases or deletes the backing machine; for delegated runners it tears down the underlying sandbox; for static `provider=ssh` hosts it attempts connection cleanup and removes the local claim without stopping or deleting the machine unless [power hooks](../providers/ssh.md#power-hooks) are configured. - **stop**: The stop rule has lived in `/start` as prose: stop the **supervisor**, never a supervised child by name, never `taskkill /f /im python.exe`.
